Gumbel baseline, while maintaining strong low-FPR detectability and competitive overall generation quality. Additional token-edit stress tests further assess watermark robustness under controlled synchronization drift.

</details>


### [131] [Ontology-Driven Structural Regularization for Document-Level Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20856)
*Laura Menotti,Stefano Marchesin,Gianmaria Silvello*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出文档级关系抽取（DocRE）中一个被忽视的噪声来源——关系三元组的结构不一致性（包括本体约束违反和逻辑矛盾），并提出一个本体驱动的框架来量化并强制DocRE数据集的结构一致性，实验表明在训练中强制结构良构性可显著减少逻辑矛盾并提升泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DocRE依赖成本高昂的人工标注数据集，而大规模的远程监督资源（如DocRED distant）因噪声问题未被充分利用。现有研究主要关注远程监督中的实体匹配或对齐噪声，却忽视了关系三元组内部的结构不一致性问题（如违反本体约束和逻辑矛盾），这是一个关键的盲区。

Method: 提出一个本体驱动的框架：首先量化DocRE数据集中的结构不一致性（包括本体约束违反和逻辑矛盾），然后通过在训练过程中强制结构良构性（structural well-formedness）来对模型施加结构正则化，从而利用大规模远程监督数据。

Result: 分析发现DocRED distant中存在大量结构噪声，且这些不一致性会传播到模型预测中。在训练中强制执行结构良构性可显著减少逻辑矛盾，并一致性地提升模型的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 结构一致性是DocRE中缺失的监督维度，结构正则化是利用大规模远程监督数据的有效策略。

Abstract: Document-Level Relation Extraction (DocRE) relies heavily on costly manually annotated datasets, while large distant supervision resources such as DocRED distant remain underexploited due to noise. We show that a critical yet overlooked source of noise lies in structural inconsistencies within relational triples, including violations of ontology constraints and logical contradictions.
  We introduce an ontology-driven framework to quantify and enforce structural consistency in DocRE datasets. Our analysis reveals substantial structural noise in DocRED distant and demonstrates that such inconsistencies propagate to model predictions. Enforcing structural well-formedness during training significantly reduces logical contradictions and consistently improves generalization performance. These findings establish structural consistency as a missing axis of supervision in DocRE and highlight structural regularization as an effective strategy for leveraging distant data at scale.

</details>


### [132] [KREL: Automatic Medical Coding via Knowledge-Guided Reasoning over Clinical Evidence with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20887)
*Xubin Chen,Yipeng Zhou,Wen Sun,Chengkai Huang,Xiaoming Fu,Quan Z. Sheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: KREL是一个利用LLM进行临床文本理解与推理，同时将外部ICD编码指南作为结构化知识集成的框架，通过领域知识与LLM推理的紧密耦合，减少幻觉并提升编码合规性，在基准数据集上优于现有PLM和LLM基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动医疗编码面临三大挑战：临床笔记长度过长影响有效解读、ICD标签空间庞大、复杂的编码规则未被LLM显式捕捉。现有PLM方法将其视为极端多标签分类问题，而LLM方法则作为生成或多步推理，但均未有效解决上述挑战。

Method: 提出KREL框架，利用LLM进行临床文本理解和推理，同时将外部ICD编码指南作为结构化知识集成到推理过程中，实现领域知识与LLM推理的紧密耦合，从而减少幻觉并提高编码标准的合规性。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，KREL在性能上一致优于强PLM基线和最先进的LLM基线方法。

Conclusion: 将外部编码指南作为结构化知识注入LLM推理过程是提升自动医疗编码准确性和合规性的有效途径，KREL框架验证了该设计理念的可行性。

Abstract: Automatic Medical Coding (AMC), which assigns standardized International Classification of Diseases (ICD) codes to clinical notes, is essential for medical reimbursement, quality reporting, and clinical research. Existing pre-trained language model (PLM)-based methods typically formulate AMC as an extreme multi-label classification problem over a predefined code set, while recent large language model (LLM)-based approaches instead frame it as generation or multi-step reasoning. However, key challenges remain, including the extreme length of clinical notes that hinders effective interpretation, the vast ICD label space, and complex coding rules that are not explicitly captured by LLMs. In this work, we propose Knowledge-Guided Reasoning over Clinical Evidence with LLMs (KREL), a framework that leverages LLMs for clinical text understanding and reasoning while integrating external ICD coding guidelines as structured knowledge. This design enables tight coupling between domain knowledge and LLM reasoning, reducing hallucinations and improving compliance with coding standards. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that KREL consistently outperforms strong PLM-based and state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [133] [ForeDreamer: A Self-Evolving Dual-Agent Memory Architecture for Future Event Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20920)
*Linhao Zhong,Zongze Du,Linyu Wu,Yu Bo,Hourong Li,Chenchen Jing,Hao Chen,Yuling Xi,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ForeDreamer，一个自进化的双智能体框架，用于开放网络未来事件预测。它将原始网页证据转化为结构化记忆，分离事实记忆（当前预测的问题特定证据状态）与经验记忆（跨预测场景积累的智能体经验），并通过主智能体负责搜索与预测、记忆处理子智能体负责转换证据，同时通过两条路径进化经验记忆来改善预测决策和事实记忆构建。在Prophet Arena和FutureX上的实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放网络未来事件预测要求智能体从嘈杂、冗余且不完整的证据中提炼可靠信号。现有检索/记忆机制要么直接将检索信息喂给智能体，要么依赖简单记忆函数（如存储和重用先前信息），不足以应对开放网页预测任务。因此需要将原始网页证据在预测前转化为结构化记忆，使智能体基于提炼后的、针对问题特定的证据进行推理。

Method: ForeDreamer采用双智能体架构，将事实记忆（当前预测的问题特定证据状态）与经验记忆（跨预测场景积累的智能体经验）分离。主智能体负责搜索和预测，记忆处理子智能体使用专用工具将搜索结果转换为事实记忆。经验记忆通过两条路径进化，同时改善预测决策和事实记忆构建。

Result: 在Prophet Arena和FutureX数据集上的实验证明了ForeDreamer的有效性。

Conclusion: 将原始网页证据在预测前转化为结构化记忆，并采用双智能体框架分离事实记忆与经验记忆、通过自进化机制持续改进，能有效提升开放网页未来事件预测的性能。

Abstract: Open-web future event prediction requires agents to distill reliable signals from noisy, redundant, and incomplete evidence. Existing retrieval/memory mechanisms directly feed retrieved information to agents or rely on simple memory functions such as storing and reusing prior information for prediction, leaving them insufficient for open-web forecasting. We propose to transform raw web evidence into structured memory before prediction, enabling agents to reason over distilled, question-specific evidence rather than noisy retrieval results. This paper presents ForeDreamer, a self-evolving dual-agent framework for managing memory over open-web evidence. ForeDreamer separates factual memory, a question-specific evidence state for the current forecast, from experiential memory, persistent agent experience accumulated across forecasting episodes. It uses a main agent for search and prediction, and a memory-processing subagent to convert search results into factual memory with dedicated tools. ForeDreamer further evolves experiential memory through two tracks, improving both forecasting decisions and factual-memory construction. Experiments on Prophet Arena and FutureX demonstrate the effectiveness of ForeDreamer. Project page: https://zhongzero.github.io/ForeDreamer

</details>


### [134] [Source-Free MT Evaluation Is Not MT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20925)
*Baban Gain,Ramakrishna Appicharla,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文主张机器翻译评估中充分性（adequacy）必须以源句子为评判标准，参考译文只是一种辅助证据而非金标准。作者呼吁将无参考的质量估计（QE）重新定位为源语言充分性评估的首选方法，并呼吁设计混合指标时明确优先考虑源-假设忠实度，参考译文仅作补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MT评估中参考译文类指标占据主导地位，但这一做法在结构上是不完整的：参考译文只是源句的一种可能呈现方式，可能引入偏差、欠指定或错误。质量估计（QE）虽然更忠实于充分性定义，但相关度往往低于参考类指标，因而被降级为参考不可用时的后备方案。作者旨在纠正这一认知偏差。

Method: 本文主要通过论证和实证观察展开：首先论证充分性必须相对于源句子而非参考译文来评判；其次论证仅当裁判将参考当作辅助证据而非主要标准时，源-参考-假设三方评估才公平；并通过观察指出现有混合指标对参考的依赖度远高于对源的依赖度。

Result: 作者指出现有混合指标在源与参考之间高度偏向参考，说明当前的混合评估实践在结构上尚未实现源优先的充分性判断。据此提出两个呼吁：将QE重新定位为源语言充分性评估的主要方法；设计混合指标时使源-假设忠实度占据主导地位，参考仅作补充证据。

Conclusion: 任何移除源句、或允许参考支配源的评估协议，在结构上都不完整，不足以评估翻译充分性。应转变范式，将QE作为源基于充分性评估的主流工具，而非参考缺失时的权宜之策。

Abstract: Reference-based metrics remain the standard choice in machine translation evaluation, partly because quality estimation methods often correlate less well with human judgments. As a result, source-free, reference-based evaluation has become the practical norm, even though it is unfaithful to the definition of translation adequacy and unfair to systems whose outputs preserve the source meaning while differing from the reference. This paper argues that adequacy must be judged with respect to the source. A reference is only one possible rendering of the source and may introduce bias, under-specification, or errors. We further argue that source-reference-hypothesis evaluation is fair only when the judge treats the reference as auxiliary evidence rather than as the primary standard. Otherwise, even source-aware evaluation can reduce adequacy to preference towards reference. We show the existing hybrid metrics are highly reliant on reference compared to source. Our argument is not that all automatic MT metrics fail to use the source. Rather, we argue that any evaluation protocol that removes the source, or allows the reference to dominate the source, is structurally incomplete for adequacy evaluation. However, existing MT papers generally prefer reference-based metrics and use QE metrics only when reference is unavailable. We therefore call for QE to be reframed as a primary approach to source-grounded adequacy evaluation, rather than as a fallback motivated by missing references. We further call for hybrid metrics whose designs explicitly prioritize source--hypothesis faithfulness while using references only as complementary evidence.

</details>


### [135] [MentorPulse: Refreshing Cross-Model Latent Guidance for Long-Form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20927)
*Ziwu Liu,Guozhong Li,Chen Qiu,Weiyang Kong,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 MentorPulse 框架，通过压缩导师状态到有容量上限的槽记忆、增量处理新生成 token、并通过门控交叉注意力更新学生读取的记忆（无需重置学生 KV 缓存），解决跨模型潜在引导在长文本生成中因静态信号失效的问题。在 13 个数据集上平均缩小了 52.2% 的导师-学生差距，优于 C2C、T2T 和等预算 LoRA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模型潜在引导方法（冻结大导师编码输入，冻结小学生在给定信号下生成）假设引导信号在输出增长过程中持续有效，但论文发现这一假设在长文本生成中不成立。在多轮指令跟随任务中，静态引导使 4B 学生的约束满足度比无引导基线低 2.5 点，说明需要动态刷新引导信号。然而训练-免刷新每 16 个 token 刷新只会改变记忆内容（需重置 KV 缓存），成本高且恢复的收益有限（2.0 点增益）。因此需要在保持实用成本的前提下让引导信号保持新鲜。

Method: 提出 MentorPulse 框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 将导师状态压缩到有容量上限的槽记忆（capped slot memory）中；(2) 增量处理新生成的 token 以更新记忆；(3) 通过门控交叉注意力（gated cross-attention）更新学生读取的记忆，且无需重置学生的 KV 缓存。同时提出 Windowed Refresh Training 训练策略，使桥接模块适配前缀条件化的记忆。还设计了一个轻量级的读模式检查（read-pattern check）用于在部署前预测性能增益。

Result: 在 13 个数据集的宏平均上，MentorPulse 缩小了 52.2% 的导师-学生差距，优于 C2C、T2T 和等预算 LoRA，且在长输出上收益最大。在来自三个模型家族的 11 个导师-学生配对中全部表现最优，且随能力差距增大优势收窄。实测成本分析确定了在长输出上优于文本引导的刷新间隔。

Conclusion: 跨模型潜在引导在长文本生成中需要动态刷新才能维持有效性，MentorPulse 以实用成本实现了引导信号的持续更新，在多种模型配对和数据集上系统性优于现有方法，并提供了部署前的增益预测手段。

Abstract: Cross-model latent guidance lets a frozen large mentor encode an input once and a frozen small student generate from the resulting signal. Existing methods keep this signal fixed, assuming it stays useful as the output grows; we show this fails in long-form generation. On multi-turn instruction following, static guidance pushes a 4B student's constraint satisfaction 2.5 points below its no-guidance baseline; a training-free refresh every 16 tokens changes only the memory content and restores a 2.0-point gain over that baseline. We propose MentorPulse to keep guidance fresh at practical cost: it compresses mentor states into a capped slot memory, incrementally processes newly generated tokens, and updates the memory that the student reads through gated cross-attention without resetting the student's KV cache. Windowed Refresh Training exposes the bridge to prefix-conditioned memory. Across thirteen datasets, MentorPulse closes 52.2% of the mentor-student gap on macro average, outperforming C2C, T2T, and equal-budget LoRA, with the largest gains on long outputs. It performs best on all eleven mentor-student pairs from three model families, with margins that narrow as the capability gap grows, and a lightweight read-pattern check predicts the gain before deployment. Measured costs identify refresh intervals that dominate text guidance on long outputs.

</details>


### [136] [Quantization-Aware Healing: A Practical Recipe for Recovering Compressed, 4-Bit LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20953)
*Bakbergen Ryskulov,Iker García-Ferrero,David Montero,David Jansen,Ali Hashemi,Jezabel R. Garcia,Antonio Tiene,Román Orús*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出量化感知修复（QAH）方法，用于修复经过结构压缩和4比特量化的大语言模型。该方法直接从原始未压缩模型中蒸馏出4比特学生模型，相比传统的量化感知训练（QAT）更快收敛、更稳定，并在GPT-OSS 120B到60B MXFP4管道上使学生模型在9个基准中的7个上匹配或超越其bfloat16源模型，同时节省约4倍权重内存和一半参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本部署大语言模型需要同时进行结构压缩（减少参数）和4比特量化，但这会严重损害推理、数学、编码和长上下文能力，需要恢复（healing）阶段。默认的QAT方法收敛缓慢且在峰值后崩溃，因此需要一个更高效、更稳定的修复方案。

Method: 提出量化感知修复（QAH）：由于结构压缩后的模型从未以全精度独立训练过，其bfloat16检查点本质上是蒸馏恢复的近似模型，因此QAH直接从未压缩的原始模型中蒸馏4比特学生模型，替代传统的硬标签重拟合方案（QAT）。

Result: 在GPT-OSS 120B到60B MXFP4管道上，QAH学生模型在7/9基准中匹配或超越其bfloat16源模型，权重内存约为原来的1/4，参数量为教师的一半；与匹配的QAT基线相比，约快7倍达到可比峰值，且在持续训练下保持稳定，无需手动调整早停。

Conclusion: QAH是一种无需数周超参数搜索即可部署的恢复配方，同时报告了部署经验（包括分布式训练后端之间可复现的质量差距），模型以Hypernova-60B开源形式发布。

Abstract: Serving large language models cheaply increasingly means shipping models that are both structurally compressed to a fraction of their parameters and quantized to 4 bits. Together these steps degrade reasoning, mathematics, coding, and long-context behavior enough to require a recovery, or healing, stage before deployment. The default recipe, quantization-aware training (QAT), re-fits the compressed, quantized model to hard labels; in our pipeline it converged slowly and collapsed past its peak. We adopted Quantization-Aware Healing (QAH) instead. Because a structurally compressed model is never independently trained at full precision, its bfloat16 checkpoint is a distillation-recovered approximation of the original; QAH distills the 4-bit student directly from the original, uncompressed model. On a GPT-OSS 120B to 60B to MXFP4 pipeline, the QAH student matches or beats its bfloat16 source on 7 of 9 benchmarks at roughly 4 times less weight memory and half the teacher's parameter count, and is released open-weight as Hypernova-60B. Against a matched QAT baseline it reaches a comparable peak about 7 times faster and stays stable under continued training, without hand-tuned early stopping. We also report deployment lessons, including a large, reproducible quality gap between distributed-training backends. Our aim is a recipe deployable without a multi-week hyper-parameter search.

</details>


### [137] [Extractive Summarization for Arabic Documents Using SAraBERT with a Semantic Siamese Similarity Evaluation Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20964)
*Sami Shames El Deen,Mariette Awad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAraBERT，一种增强版的AraBERT，通过引入句子间Transformer层用于抽取式摘要任务，并提出Semantic Siamese Similarity作为新的评估指标来衡量文本间的语义相似度，确保摘要覆盖文档核心思想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升阿拉伯语文本的抽取式摘要质量，并解决传统评估指标（如BLEU、ROUGE）无法充分反映摘要对文档核心思想覆盖度的问题。

Method: 在AraBERT基础上增加句子间（inter-sentence）Transformer层以捕捉句子间关系，用于抽取式摘要；提出Semantic Siamese Similarity评估指标，通过孪生网络结构度量两个文本输入之间的语义相似度。

Result: 在BLEU、ROUGE和Semantic Siamese Similarity三种指标上对SAraBERT及相关已有模型进行了验证，仿真结果表明所提模型有效。

Conclusion: SAraBERT在阿拉伯语抽取式摘要任务上表现出有效性，新提出的Semantic Siamese Similarity指标能够更好地衡量摘要对文档主要思想的覆盖程度，研究为后续工作提供了动力和方向。

Abstract: In this research, we introduce SAraBERT, an enhanced version of AraBERT which proposes inter-sentence transformer layers for extractive summarization tasks. To ensure that the summaries generated by SAraBERT achieve a high coverage of the document's main ideas, we propose Semantic Siamese Similarity, a novel evaluation metric that measures the level of similarity between two text inputs. We validated using BLEU, ROUGE, and Semantic Siamese similarity on Sarabert and published related models. Simulation results showed the effectiveness of our proposed model and motivate follow on research.

</details>


### [138] [Target-Aware Calibration Data Selection for Preserving Uncertainty in Quantized Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21019)
*Zhen Yang,Sizai Hou,Kaiwen Zheng,Yaofang Liu,Liang He,Yixuan Chen,Kangning Cui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"保疑量化"(Doubt-Preserving Quantization, DPQ)，将量化校准数据选择形式化为一个目标依赖的不确定性保持问题。通过分布保持风险和边界保持风险进行形式化定义，并给出混合不匹配论证解释为何单一校准策略无法适配所有部署目标。DPQ利用全精度预测构建由高疑虑样本与通用锚点组成的目标对齐校准混合集。实验覆盖8个语言模型、9个NLP基准和22种对比方法，发现最优固定配方随保持目标而变化：DPQ-r75在SQuAD2可回答性边界保持上表现最佳，而DPQ-r50、仅置信度及仅熵变体则更擅长保持多选QA行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化和部署LLM时，其置信度、边界和放弃(abstention)等不确定性行为几乎不被作为首要优化目标。已有工作主要优化精度导向的压缩指标，或在量化后调整得分。然而不同部署场景关注输入分布的不同区域，校准数据选择需要针对特定全精度得分行为进行定制，而非作为固定的量化细节处理。

Method: 将校准数据选择形式化为目标依赖的不确定性保持问题；提出分布保持风险与边界保持风险两种形式化目标；通过混合不匹配论证说明单一校准配方的局限性；提出DPQ——一种轻量级量化前配方族，利用全精度预测构建由高疑虑示例和通用锚点组成的目标对齐校准混合集，包含r75、r50、仅置信度、仅熵等多种变体。

Result: 在8个语言模型、9个NLP基准和22种对比方法上，DPQ-r75在SQuAD2可回答性边界保持方面领先；而DPQ-r50、仅置信度和仅熵等较温和或单信号变体在保持广泛多选QA行为方面表现更优。最优固定配方随保持目标变化，验证了方法的核心假设。

Conclusion: 校准数据应根据部署需要保持的特定全精度得分行为来选择，而非作为固定量化细节处理。不同部署目标需要不同的校准数据配方，DPQ提供了一套可灵活组合的轻量级方案来满足这一需求。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to deploy large language models, but its effect on uncertainty behavior, such as confidence, margins, and abstention, is rarely treated as a primary objective. We frame calibration-data selection for quantization as a target-dependent uncertainty-preservation problem. Different deployments emphasize different regions of the input distribution, yet prior work mainly optimizes accuracy-oriented compression metrics or adjusts scores after quantization. We formalize this goal with distributional and boundary preservation risks, and provide a simple mixture-mismatch argument explaining why no single calibration recipe should be expected to fit all targets. We introduce Doubt-Preserving Quantization (DPQ), a lightweight pre-quantization recipe family that uses full-precision predictions to construct target-aligned calibration mixtures of high-doubt examples and generic anchors. Across 8 language models, 9 NLP benchmarks, and 22 comparison methods, the leading fixed recipe changes with the preservation target: DPQ-r75 leads on SQuAD2 answerability-boundary preservation, while milder or single-signal variants, including DPQ-r50, confidence-only, and entropy-only, better preserve broad multiple-choice QA behavior. These results show that calibration data should be selected for the specific full-precision score behavior a deployment needs to preserve, rather than treated as a fixed quantization detail.

</details>


### [139] [Free-Text Evaluation of LLMs for 5G Domain Knowledge and Fault Analysis using LLM-as-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21021)
*Rishiraj Sengupta,Sotiris Chatzimiltis,Mohammad Shojafar,Xiatian Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三个轻量级边缘可部署LLM（Claude-Haiku-4.5、GPT-5.4-Mini、Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite）在自由文本格式下对5G领域知识与故障分析的诊断能力，跨越三个基准测试。所有模型在故障诊断上达到90%以上准确率，但零样本3GPP和O-RAN规范回忆率低于60%。多裁判LLM评分的一致性均不低于0.90，验证了LLM-as-Judge方法的可靠性。Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite在准确率与推理成本/延迟之间取得最佳平衡，最适合生产部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G及未来6G网络的真实故障分析需要领域专家解读自由文本诊断信息（根因解释与推荐操作）。LLMs提供自动化方案，但轻量级、边缘可部署模型能否胜任深度自由文本诊断仍是开放问题。现有基准依赖固定答案的MCQ，缺乏对开放文本生成能力的评估，因此需要转向自由文本生成范式并建立可靠的验证框架。

Method: 作者评估三个轻量级LLM在三个基准（TeleQNA ORAN FT、5G-Faults FT、TeleInter FT）上的自由文本5G领域知识与故障分析能力。采用三个独立的前沿LLM作为裁判对输出进行评分，并通过成对裁判间一致性测量作为LLM-as-Judge方法论的经验检验。

Result: 所有三个模型在故障诊断上达到至少90%准确率；零样本3GPP和O-RAN规范回忆率是关键短板，所有模型均低于60%；所有运行中裁判间一致性均值至少为0.90，表明多裁判LLM评分对开放电信响应产生一致且可复现的评分。Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite在竞争力准确率与最低推理成本和延迟间取得最佳平衡。

Conclusion: 轻量级边缘可部署LLM在5G故障诊断的自由文本生成任务中表现优异（≥90%准确率），但需提升对3GPP和O-RAN规范知识的零样本回忆能力。多裁判LLM评分框架能提供一致可复现的评估。Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite因其效率优势被推荐为生产电信部署的最佳候选模型。

Abstract: Real-world fault analysis in 5G and emerging 6G networks demands domain expertise to analyze free-text diagnostics, including root-cause explanations and recommended actions. LLMs have emerged as a promising approach to automating this, yet whether lightweight, edge-deployable models are capable of performing in-depth free-text diagnostics remains an open question. While existing benchmarks rely on restrictive MCQs with fixed answer keys, this paper evaluates 5G domain understanding and fault analysis in a free-text generation format. Transitioning to this paradigm requires evaluating lightweight, edge-deployable AI models on open-ended diagnostic reasoning, alongside a dependable framework to validate these text outputs at scale. To address this we evaluate three lightweight LLMs, Claude-Haiku-4.5, GPT-5.4-Mini, and Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite, on free-text 5G domain knowledge and fault-analysis tasks across three benchmarks, TeleQNA ORAN FT, 5G-Faults FT, and TeleInter FT. Three independent frontier judges score outputs, and pairwise inter-judge agreement is measured as an empirical test of the LLM-as-Judge methodology. All three models reach at least 90% accuracy on fault diagnosis, while zero-shot recall of 3GPP and O-RAN specifications remains the critical gap, with all models scoring below 60%. Mean inter-judge agreement is at least 0.90 across all runs, indicating that multi-judge LLM scoring produces consistent, reproducible grades for open-ended telecom responses. Operationally, Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite offers the best efficiency trade-off, combining competitive accuracy with the lowest inference cost and latency, making it the most suitable candidate for production telecom deployments.

</details>


### [140] [Scaling Unsupervised Word Alignment to Documents via Structural Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21023)
*Michelle Wastl,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CTFAlign和MDPAlign两种轻量级、无需训练的文档级词对齐方法，采用从粗到细的搜索空间限制策略，在无需句子分割或句对齐的情况下直接在完整文档上操作，将词对齐错误率从0.412降至0.326，并带来下游任务（文档级翻译覆盖评估和语义差异识别）的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统词对齐研究集中在句子层面，但许多跨语言任务需要文档级别的对应关系。现有的多语言嵌入模型能编码长输入，但将句子级对齐算法直接应用于文档会导致性能下降，因此需要专门针对文档级的词对齐方法。

Method: 提出两种方法：(1) CTFAlign：采用从粗到细的细化策略，将对齐搜索空间限制在语义相似区域；(2) MDPAlign：更简单的替代方案，通过对角线先验（main diagonal prior）按位置约束对齐。两种方法都直接作用于完整文档，不依赖句子分割或句子对齐。

Result: 在六个语言对（覆盖不同类型学距离、资源丰富度和文档长度的组合）上评估，三个模型平均后CTFAlign将词对齐错误率从0.412降低到0.326。增益可传递到下游任务，改善文档级翻译覆盖率评估和语义差异识别。相关代码和Python包已公开。

Conclusion: CTFAlign和MDPAlign证明可以在不需要句子分割的情况下实现有效的文档级词对齐，且方法轻量、无需训练。该工作推动了文档级跨语言对齐的研究，并为下游多语言任务提供了可复用的开源工具。

Abstract: Word alignment has traditionally been studied between sentences, but many cross-lingual tasks increasingly require correspondences across full documents. While recent multilingual embedding models can encode long inputs, we show that applying algorithms designed for sentences directly to documents leads to performance degradation. To address this, we introduce CTFAlign, a lightweight, training-free approach for document-level word alignment. CTFAlign applies a coarse-to-fine refinement strategy that restricts the alignment search space to semantically similar regions. Additionally, we introduce MDPAlign, a simpler alternative that constrains alignments by position with a main diagonal prior. Both approaches operate directly on full documents without relying on sentence segmentation or sentence alignment. We evaluate these methods across six language pairs varying in typological distance, resourcedness, and document length. Averaged over three models, CTFAlign reduces word alignment error rate from 0.412 to 0.326. These gains transfer downstream, leading to improvements in document-level translation coverage evaluation and recognition of semantic differences. We release CTFAlign as a Python package and make the code and data to reproduce our experiments publicly available.

</details>


### [141] [Evidence-Consistent Generative Detection under Scenario-Level Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21043)
*San Kim,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究社交工程诈骗（短信和语音钓鱼）检测中的场景级分布外（SL-OOD）泛化问题，指出现有分布内评估会高估模型的鲁棒性。通过将整个攻击场景从训练中排除，作者发现高分布内性能并不能可靠预测场景级分布外鲁棒性，并将这一差距归因于"场景记忆化"（对场景特定词汇/实体线索的依赖而非决策相关证据）。为此提出ECoG框架，结合证据跨度监督与理由-标签一致性目标进行训练，在0.5B解码器上使OOD困难样本的Macro-F1提升3.22点，预测与生成理由不一致比例下降4.22点，与参考证据跨度的token级重叠提升8.38点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的分布内评估在训练和测试数据共享反复出现的任务特定模式或表面线索时会高估模型鲁棒性。在社交工程诈骗检测中，攻击者可以在保留恶意意图的同时改变场景、冒充实体或措辞，因此模型可能依赖场景特有线索而非决策相关证据。作者希望评估模型能否在遭遇未见攻击场景时依然保持检测能力。

Method: 提出了场景级分布外（SL-OOD）检测设定，将整个攻击场景从训练集中排除但保持标签空间不变。在此基础上提出ECoG（evidence-consistent generative）框架，结合证据跨度监督（evidence-span supervision）与理由-标签一致性目标（rationale-label consistency objective）进行训练，使模型生成的解释性理由与预测标签保持一致。

Result: 高分布内性能不能可靠预测分布外鲁棒性（跨特征、编码器和解码器基线均如此）。在0.5B解码器上，ECoG相对无一致性正则化的同骨干网络，Macro-F1在OOD困难实例上提升3.22点，预测理由支持相反标签的占比下降4.22点，与参考证据跨度的token级重叠提升8.38点；预测-理由不一致性的降低在四种解码器骨干上均一致。

Conclusion: 紧凑型生成式检测器能从证据监督和理由-标签一致性训练中获益，从而在社交工程场景偏移下更好地泛化。分布内评估的高分可能掩盖模型对场景特有线索的记忆化，而ECoG通过实证证据一致性约束缓解了这一问题。

Abstract: Conventional in-distribution evaluation can overestimate robustness when training and test data share recurring task-specific patterns or surface cues. This risk is especially relevant in social-engineering fraud detection, where attackers can preserve malicious intent while changing the scenario, impersonated entity, or wording. We study this problem as scenario-level out-of-distribution (SL-OOD) detection for SMS and voice phishing, where entire attack scenarios are held out from training while the label space remains fixed. This setting tests whether models can generalize to unseen attack scenarios using decision-relevant evidence rather than familiar scenario-specific cues. Using this SL-OOD evaluation, we find that high in-distribution performance does not reliably predict held-out robustness across feature-, encoder-, and decoder-based baselines. We interpret this gap as scenario memorization: reliance on recurring scenario-specific lexical or entity cues rather than decision-relevant evidence. We propose ECoG, an evidence-consistent generative framework that combines evidence-span supervision with a rationale-label consistency objective during training. On the 0.5B decoder, relative to the same backbone trained without consistency regularization, ECoG raises Macro-F1 on OOD challenging instances by 3.22 points, reduces the share of predictions whose generated rationale supports the opposite label by 4.22 points, and increases token-level overlap with reference evidence spans by 8.38 points; the reduction in prediction-rationale inconsistency is consistent across four decoder backbones. These results suggest that compact generative detectors can benefit from evidence supervision and rationale-label consistency under social-engineering shift.

</details>


### [142] [PromptResponse: Optimizing Prompts for LLM Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21074)
*Erik Thureck,Robert Kühnen,Tim Jacobowitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一个名为"PromptResponse"的受控研究，通过五种语义相同但语法不同的编码提示变体（基线、JSON、Markdown、YAML和LLM调优版本）在HumanEval数据集上的8200次执行中，系统性地研究了提示格式化和基于LLM的调优对代码性能、效率与稳定性的影响。研究发现一致的格式化（尤其是JSON）能提升生成效率和语法稳定性，而LLM调优的提示反而显著降低了任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在研究和软件开发流程中日益广泛使用，但其输出对输入提示的变化高度敏感。然而，目前缺乏对提示格式化与LLM调优如何系统性地影响编码任务结果的受控研究，因此需要填补这一空白以指导实际应用。

Method: 以HumanEval数据集为基础，构造五种语义一致但语法不同的提示变体（baseline、JSON、Markdown、YAML以及由LLM调优的版本），使用GPT-4o进行超过8200次执行，评估各变体在任务性能、生成效率、稳定性等维度上的表现，并发布数据集变体与评估流水线。

Result: 一致的格式化（特别是JSON格式）能提升生成效率和语法稳定性，且在任务性能上有小幅提升；相反，LLM调优的提示在任务性能上显著下降，且在其他维度上没有明显改善。

Conclusion: 低成本的格式重排本身就能带来可衡量的改进，而LLM提示调优则需要考虑模型对齐问题。论文最后提出了一系列基于结果的实际建议，并发布了数据集变体和评估流水线供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in research workflows and software development pipelines, yet their output remains sensitive to input prompt variations. This paper presents $\unicode{x00AB}$PromptResponse$\unicode{x00BB}$, a controlled study examining how formatting and LLM-based tuning of coding task prompts affect the resulting code's performance, efficiency, and stability. Using five semantically identical yet syntactically distinct variants of the HumanEval dataset$\unicode{x2014}$baseline, JSON, Markdown, YAML, and an LLM-tuned version$\unicode{x2014}$we had GPT-4o solve its coding problems over 8200$\unicode{x00A0}$executions. Our results show that consistent formatting$\unicode{x2014}$especially JSON$\unicode{x2014}$improves generation efficiency and syntactic stability, with minor gains in task performance. Conversely, the LLM-tuned prompts resulted in significantly degraded task performance without significant improvements in any other dimension. These findings suggest that low-effort reformatting alone can yield measurable improvements, while tuning must account for model alignment. We conclude our work with providing a set of practical recommendations informed by our results as well as releasing our dataset variants and evaluation pipeline for future work.

</details>


### [143] [Jokes Aside: Measuring the Semantic Distance of Double Meanings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21087)
*Fabio De Ponte*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用上下文嵌入向量（OpenAI text-embedding-3-small和MiniLM all-MiniLM-L6-v2）重新审视了Petrovic和Matthews（2013）提出的"I like my X like I like my Y, Z"句式笑料生成模型中的幽默预测指标，在三个数据集（JokeJudger、Expunations、rJokes）上测试了obviousness、compatibility、comparison及新引入的symmetry指标。结果显示基于这些指标训练的模型预测幽默评分的准确率（最高57.1%）低于基线（61.5%），但symmetry指标与高评分笑话持续相关，可能是幽默的必要但非充分属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大型语言模型和上下文嵌入向量为计算幽默研究提供了新工具，使研究者有机会重新审视和修正早期的幽默生成假设。本文旨在利用词嵌入技术对此前基于Ngrams和Word2Vector提出的幽默指标进行验证和扩展。

Method: 使用两种嵌入模型（OpenAI text-embedding-3-small和MiniLM）在三个数据集上收集词向量，重新实现五个指标中的三个（obviousness、compatibility、comparison），并首次引入新指标symmetry（定义为Z与X和Y的接近程度）。Expunations和rJokes数据集通过添加配对句子来捕获笑话核心歧义表达在两种不同含义下的语义。

Result: 基于所提出指标训练的模型在幽默评分预测上表现不佳：JokeJudger上最好模型准确率为57.1%，低于61.5%的基线；Expunations和rJokes上表现更低。但symmetry指标始终与更高评价的笑话相关。

Conclusion: 已有基于词嵌入的幽默指标不足以单独准确预测幽默程度，但symmetry指标可能捕捉了幽默的必要（虽不充分）属性，为未来幽默计算研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models have significantly enriched the toolkit for computational humor research, particularly in the automated generation of jokes and puns. A key innovation, contextual embedding vectors, offers new opportunities to revisit and refine earlier hypotheses. Notably, Petrovic and Matthews (2013) proposed a joke generation model based on the scheme "I like my X like I like my Y, Z" (e.g. "I like my ice like I like my dreams, crushed"). They suggested that joke hilarity increases with: a) frequent association of Z with X and Y, b) rarity of Z, c) ambiguity of Z, and d) meaning distance between X and Y. Building on this, Winters et al. (2019) proposed a set of metrics, based on Google Ngrams and Word2Vector. In this work, three out of their five metrics are revisited with word embeddings: obviousness, compatibility, and comparison. Another measure, symmetry, defined as closeness of Z to both X and Y, is introduced here for the first time. Two models were used to collect the embedding vectors (OpenAI text-embedding-3-small and MiniLM all-MiniLM-L6-v2) on three datasets: JokeJudger, Expunations, and rJokes. The last two datasets, Expunations, and rJokes, were expanded by adding paired sentences that captured the ambiguous expression at the core of each joke in its two different meanings. Results revealed that models trained on the proposed metrics performed poorly in predicting humor ratings: on JokeJudger, the best model achieved 57.1% accuracy, below the 61.5% baseline, while performance on Expunations and rJokes was even lower. Nevertheless, the symmetry metric seems consistently associated with higher-rated jokes, suggesting it may capture a necessary -though not sufficient- property of humor.

</details>


### [144] [When the Feature Pool Goes Algorithmic: Extending Mufwene's Ecology of Language Evolution to LLM-Mediated Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21088)
*Kunmei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大型语言模型（LLMs）视为"分布中介体"，扩展Mufwene的语言演化生态模型。LLMs聚合、转换并大规模重新分发人类语言分布，通过"算法重加权"改变竞争性语言变体到达人类选择者的相对频率。但该过程不必然导致收敛，人类社会评价仍起决定性作用，AI相关形式可能扩散、定型、被标记为"AI样"后遭回避，或根本未能扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mufwene的生态学模型将语言演化定位于个体语库（idiolects）贡献的变体间的竞争以及说话者从互动中可得的语言材料中进行选择的过程。大语言模型的出现复杂化了这一架构，需要解释LLMs在这一语言生态系统中扮演的角色——它们并非取代人类选择，而是改变人类可选择的分布本身。

Method: 本文采用理论论证与概念构建的方法。作者提出"算法重加权"概念，将LLMs定位为分布中介体（distributional mediators），具体机制包括三个环节：聚合跨人类群体的语言产出、通过训练和对齐（post-training）转化其分布、大规模重新分发模型特有输出。文章还结合新兴的实证证据（模型特有语言特征、词汇采纳研究）对理论路径进行部分验证。

Result: 文章产出五个可检验的预测方向：语言采纳（uptake）、模型版本效应、收敛性、社会反转（social reversal）。现有证据部分支持该路径，但未确立必然收敛。文章将Mufewene的特征池生态学（feature-pool ecology）向前延伸到说话者选择的上游环节。

Conclusion: LLMs应被视作语言生态系统中的分布中介体而非竞争者或说话者替代品。模型介导可改变竞争变体到达人类选择者的相对频率，但人类的社会评价仍是语言演化的最终仲裁机制。AI关联形式可能走三条路径：扩散并常规化、被标记为"AI样"后遭社会回避、或从一开始就未能扩散。

Abstract: Mufwene's ecological model locates language evolution in competition among variants contributed by individual idiolects and in speakers' selection from linguistic material made available through interaction. Large language models (LLMs) complicate this architecture without requiring the locus of selection to move away from human speakers. This article argues that LLMs are best treated as distributional mediators: they aggregate language produced across human populations, transform its distribution through training and post-training, and redistribute model-specific outputs at scale. I call the resulting ecological process algorithmic reweighting of the speaker-accessible distribution: model mediation can alter the relative frequencies with which competing variants reach human selectors. Emerging evidence on model-specific linguistic profiles and lexical uptake is consistent with parts of this pathway, but does not establish inevitable convergence. Human social evaluation remains decisive: model-associated forms may diffuse and become conventionalized, become socially recognizable as 'AI-like' and subsequently avoided, or fail to diffuse in the first place. The proposal extends Mufwene's feature-pool ecology one step upstream of speaker selection and yields testable predictions about uptake, model-version effects, convergence, and social reversal.

</details>


### [145] [No PUN Intended: Plausible Unknown Names for Person-Centred LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21206)
*Dimitri Staufer,David Hartmann,Ibrahim Baroud*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PUN（Plausible Unknown Names）协议，用于系统化构建和验证"未知人名"——即具有合理名-姓结构但在受控验证下无索引的全文证据、且无歧义信号的名字。该协议结合Wikidata词条组件、网络化LLM筛选和受控搜索再验证，并在204人参与的人类研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM评估中，人名常被用作提示变量来测试事实性、隐私泄露、偏见和弃答能力。然而，当名字的证据状态（evidential status）未被控制时，测量结果可能混淆记忆、检索、名字先验和错误归因等多种因素，导致评估失真。因此需要一种系统化的方法来构建和验证"真正的未知名字"。

Method: PUN协议包含三个核心环节：（1）从Wikidata提取组件来合成具有合理名-姓形式的名字；（2）使用网络化LLM筛选（web-enabled LLM screening）进行初筛；（3）通过受控搜索再验证（controlled search revalidation）确认名字无索引的全文证据、无歧义信号。此外，报告了接受率、可复现性、消融实验，并进行了204名参与者的人类研究。

Result: 实验结果显示：被接受的名字比对照组更具"名字特征"（more name-like）；在人类研究中，参与者仅能在3%的情况下恢复出这些人名对应的人物证据。研究还报告了接受率、可复现性和消融实验（ablation）结果，并发布了包含300个名字及其对照组的数据集。

Conclusion: PUN协议提供了一种可复现且经过验证的未知人名构建方法，有助于在LLM评估中更精确地分离记忆与检索、名字先验和错误归因等混杂因素。发布的300个名字及对照数据可用于未来LLM事实性、隐私和偏见等维度的评估研究。

Abstract: Person names are widely used as prompt variables in LLM evaluations of factuality, privacy leakage, bias and abstention, but when a name's evidential status is uncontrolled, measurements may conflate memorisation, retrieval, name priors and wrong-person attribution. We operationalise an unknown name as one with plausible First-Last form, no indexed full-name evidence, and no ambiguity signals under a documented validation run, and introduce PUN (Plausible Unknown Names), a protocol for constructing and validating such names, combining Wikidata-derived components, web-enabled LLM screening, and controlled search revalidation. We report acceptance rate, reproducibility, ablations, and a 204-participant human study, finding accepted names are more name-like than controls while participants recover person evidence in only 3% of cases. We release 300 names with comparison controls.

</details>


### [146] [RARE: Decoupling Representation Steering from Expert Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21236)
*Zhibo Zhang,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RARE框架，一种针对混合专家（MoE）大语言模型的路由无关表示工程方法，通过将行为扰动投影到路由器矩阵的零空间来消除路由可见分量，并校正下游层的路由漂移，在有害性、真实性和事实编辑三个任务上显著优于传统表示工程技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示工程通过修改中间隐藏状态来控制语言模型行为，是一种轻量级方法，但直接应用于MoE模型时存在结构性不匹配。作者通过实证研究验证了这一失败模式，发现路由一致性在MoE模型中至关重要——干净路由能显著恢复转向性能，且路由对语义内容比对行为变化更敏感。

Method: RARE框架包含两个关键步骤：(1) 将任意行为扰动投影到路由器矩阵的零空间，去除路由可见分量；(2) 校正传递给所选下游层的路由漂移。作者在框架内比较了五种扰动估计器，在六个异构开源MoE模型上评估，覆盖有害性、真实性和事实编辑三个转向场景。

Result: 在有害性转向中，RARE达到53.3%的平均攻击成功率并保持67.8%的MMLU准确率，比基线实现了更强的有效性-效用权衡。TruthfulQA MC1准确率从41.0%提升至58.6%，CounterFact功效从16.8%提升至96.3%。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明路由一致性是表示工程适配MoE模型时的重要架构考量，RARE通过在路由器零空间内进行操作，有效解决了表示工程与MoE架构之间的结构性不匹配问题。

Abstract: Representation engineering offers a lightweight means of controlling language-model behavior by modifying intermediate hidden states, but its direct application to Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models introduces a structural mismatch. We first verify this failure mode through a series of empirical studies and find that preserving clean routing substantially recovers steering performance and that routing is more sensitive to semantic content than to behavioral changes under controlled content. Motivated by these findings, we introduce RARE, a router-agnostic representation engineering framework for MoE language models. RARE projects arbitrary behavioral perturbations onto the null space of the router matrix, thereby removing router-visible components, and further corrects routing drift propagated to selected downstream layers. To decide the best perturbation estimator in this framework, we evaluate five estimators on six heterogeneous open-weight MoE models across three steering scenarios: harmfulness, truthfulness, and factual editing. On harmfulness steering, RARE reaches an average attack success rate of 53.3% while retaining 67.8% MMLU accuracy, yielding a stronger aggregate effectiveness--utility trade-off than baselines. It further improves average TruthfulQA MC1 accuracy from 41.0% to 58.6% and CounterFact efficacy from 16.8% to 96.3%. These results support routing consistency as an important architectural consideration for adapting representation engineering to MoE models.

</details>


### [147] [Affective Context Amplifies Sycophancy in LLM Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21242)
*Jiayi Li,Sanjana Menon,Brett Frischmann,Shomir Wilson,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究了大型语言模型（LLM）在用户提供情感状态上下文时，其阿谀奉承（sycophancy）行为如何被调制。通过两个Reddit数据集和7个LLM，发现用户面向回复与独立评估之间存在系统性的单向分歧——模型倾向于软化或保留负面判断，且负面情感状态（尤其孤独和困扰）会放大这种分歧，表现为回避式奉承。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为对话伙伴常能接触到用户的情感状态，但情感上下文如何影响模型在主观评估性交互中的奉承行为尚不清楚。作者借鉴讨好理论（ingratiation theory），旨在量化情感上下文对模型批评性反馈的抑制效应，探讨这是否构成一个脆弱性信号。

Method: 将奉承测量为模型独立评估与用户面向回复之间的分歧。方法是通过同一内容分别呈现为第三方叙述和用户自身披露来诱发分歧，在7个LLM和两个Reddit数据集（r/AmItheAsshole和r/TrueUnpopularOpinion）上进行跨模型跨数据集实验。

Result: 发现分歧是系统性的且强烈单向：用户面向回复始终软化或保留负面/对立判断。情感上下文进一步放大这种分歧，其中负面状态（尤其是孤独和困扰）产生最大效应。模型常通过回避式奉承（转向非承诺性回复而非直接同意）来实现这种抑制。

Conclusion: 情感上下文作为脆弱性信号会抑制关键性反馈，恰恰在用户最需要的时候（负面情感状态下）模型反而最不愿给出诚实批评。这意味着LLM在情感敏感场景中可能存在系统性的反馈失效风险，需要关注其安全性和可靠性设计。

Abstract: As conversational companions, large language models (LLMs) often have access to users' emotional states. We study how this affective context modulates LLM sycophancy in subjective, evaluative interactions, where users share actions or opinions that invite feedback. Drawing on ingratiation theory, we measure sycophancy as the divergence between a model's independent evaluation and its user-facing response, elicited by presenting the same content as either a third-party account or the user's own disclosure. Across seven LLMs and two Reddit datasets (r/AmItheAsshole and r/TrueUnpopularOpinion), we find that this divergence is systematic and strongly one-directional. User-facing responses consistently soften or withhold negative or oppositional judgments. Affective context further amplifies this divergence with negative states, particularly loneliness and distress, producing the largest effects. These findings suggest that affective context functions as a vulnerability signal that suppresses critical feedback when users may need it most, often through evasive sycophancy, in which models retreat toward non-committal responses rather than outright agreement.

</details>


### [148] [Benchmarking Patent Drafting from Inventor-Style Disclosures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21249)
*Lekang Jiang,Wenjun Sun,Stephan Goetz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍Dis2Pat数据集和Patent-MAF多智能体框架，解决从早期非正式发明披露直接生成完整法律一致性专利申请的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在专利撰写任务上虽有个体任务的良好表现，但无法应对现实世界专利撰写的核心挑战——如何从早期非正式的发明人披露材料直接生成完整且法律上连贯的专利申请。先前研究主要假设后期阶段、高度结构化或已具有法律性的输入，而真实的专利工作流程始于发明人撰写的非正式、去法律化的披露文件。

Method: 构建Dis2Pat数据集，要求从发明人风格的去法律化披露直接生成完整专利申请，反映真实专利流程。提出Patent-MAF，一个可本地部署的多智能体专利撰写框架作为强基线。

Result: 基准测试表明，当前大语言模型在专利撰写上表现有限，而Patent-MAF作为强基线始终优于评估的开源模型，并与大型闭源模型保持竞争力。

Conclusion: Patent-MAF证明了面向真实专利工作流的本地可部署多智能体方案的有效性，填补了从早期发明披露到完整专利申请生成的空白。

Abstract: While recent large language models (LLMs) have achieved promising results on individual patent drafting tasks, they fundamentally fail to investigate the core challenge of real-world patent drafting: generating a complete and legally coherent patent application directly from early-stage invention materials. Prior work predominantly assumes later-stage, highly structured, or already legalistic inputs. However, real patenting workflows begin with informal, de-legalized disclosures authored by inventors. To bridge the gap, we introduce Dis2Pat, a disclosure-to-patent dataset that reflects realistic patenting workflows by requiring the generation of complete patent applications directly from inventor-style, de-legalized disclosures. Given the inherent difficulty of long-form, legally constrained patent drafting and the strong privacy requirements, we further propose a strong baseline named Patent-MAF. It is a multi-agent framework for locally deployable patent drafting. Benchmark results reveal that current LLMs exhibit limitations in patent drafting, while Patent-MAF provides a strong baseline that consistently outperforms evaluated open-source models and remains competitive with large closed-source models.

</details>


### [149] [EnSI-RAG: Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Long-Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21252)
*Xuanyu Meng,Jiashuo Sun,Jash Rajesh Parekh,Jiawei Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EnSI-RAG框架，通过构建查询无关的实体中心索引（每条记录包含实体e、类型t、语义类别k和值v），将证据定位与答案生成解耦，从而解决长文档多跳问答中传统RAG因分块切割实体关系而性能下降的问题。在Loong和Oolong数据集上平均准确率达78.24，比基线高6.62个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档问答中相关证据常跨越多个实体及其关系，而现有RAG方法以原始文本块索引并通过嵌入相似度检索，当块边界将实体与支撑证据分离或问题需要多跳推理时性能严重下降。

Method: EnSI-RAG构建查询无关的实体中心索引，每条记录包含四元组(e, t, k, v)，其中e为实体、t为类型、k为语义类别（属性/关系/方面）、v为值，同时保留指向原始源片段的链接。查询时将记录作为检索句柄，由LLM综合检索到的片段生成最终答案，实现证据定位与答案合成的分离。

Result: 在Loong和Oolong基准上，EnSI-RAG平均准确率达78.24，相对已发表基线分数高出6.62个百分点，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: EnSI-RAG通过实体级结构化索引有效解决了长文档多跳问答的检索瓶颈，同时保留可追溯的源证据，展现出优于传统分块RAG的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) over long, connected documents remains challenging because relevant evidence may span multiple entities and their relationships. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods typically index documents as raw chunks and retrieve them through embedding similarity. Their performance degrades when chunk boundaries separate entities from supporting evidence or when a question requires multi-hop reasoning across the corpus. We propose EnSI-RAG (Entity-Structure-Indexed Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a framework that constructs a query-independent, entity-centered index. Each record (e, t, k, v) represents an entity e, its type t, a semantic category k in {property, relation, aspect}, and a value v, while retaining links to the original source passages. At query time, these records serve as retrieval handles, and an LLM synthesizes the retrieved passages into the final answer. This design separates evidence localization from answer synthesis while preserving traceable source evidence. Across Loong and Oolong, EnSI-RAG achieves an average accuracy of 78.24. Relative to the published baseline scores used as references, this is 6.62 points higher, suggesting its effectiveness across these settings. The code is available at https://github.com/RamonMeng/EnSI-RAG.

</details>


### [150] [Memory Augmentation Unlocks Efficient Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21265)
*Simeng Zhang,Yilong Chen,Wenyuan Zhang,Zhenyu Zhang,Yao Chen,Junyuan Shang,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Memory-Augmented Compression（记忆增强压缩）框架，基于"上下文-生成替代定律"，通过从历史推理轨迹中构建可复用的推理记忆作为prefill端脚手架，在压缩CoT推理轨迹的同时补偿信息损失。在GSM8K、MATH、BBH和MMLU-Sci上分别比CoD提升21.4、28.0、29.5和6.61个准确率点，同时相比标准CoT获得1.14-1.49倍延迟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型依赖Chain-of-Thought（CoT）推理解决复杂任务，但冗长的推理痕迹带来大量推理开销。CoT压缩虽能缩短生成长度，但激进的压缩可能破坏逻辑连贯性并降低性能。现有的压缩方法在信息保留和效率之间面临根本性权衡，需要一种方法在压缩推理的同时保持推理质量。

Method: 首先形式化"上下文-生成替代定律"（Context-Generation Substitution Law），阐明显式推理上下文可替代部分解码时生成。基于此提出Memory-Augmented Compression，这是一种无需训练的框架：从历史推理轨迹中构建可复用的推理记忆，并在prefill端作为脚手架检索这些记忆。与传统使用原始示范不同，这些记忆总结了可复用的推理模式、关键约束和关键操作，以补偿压缩过程中丢失的信息。

Result: 在数学推理（GSM8K、MATH）、复杂推理（BBH）和科学问答（MMLU-Sci）任务上，Memory一致性地提升了基于提示的Chain-of-Draft（CoD）压缩性能，分别获得21.4、28.0、29.5和6.61个准确率点提升，同时相比标准CoT实现1.14-1.49倍延迟加速。该方法还与token级、推理痕迹级和推理状态级压缩机制兼容。进一步分析表明，性能提升来源于相关的推理记忆而非单纯增加上下文长度。

Conclusion: Memory-Augmented Compression为CoT压缩中的信息损失问题提供了一种有效的训练-free解决方案，通过检索相关推理记忆作为prefill端补强，实现了压缩效率和推理质量的双赢。该工作揭示了显式上下文与解码生成之间的替代关系，并展示了跨多种压缩粒度的兼容性。

Abstract: Large language models often rely on Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to solve complex tasks, but verbose reasoning traces introduce substantial inference overhead. CoT compression shortens generation, yet aggressive compression may disrupt logical coherence and degrade performance. We formalize this trade-off as the \textit{Context-Generation Substitution Law}, where explicit reasoning context substitutes for part of decode-time generation. Based on this principle, we propose \textit{Memory-Augmented Compression}, a training-free framework that constructs reusable reasoning memories from historical traces and retrieves them as prefill-side scaffolds. Rather than using raw demonstrations, these memories summarize reusable reasoning patterns, key constraints, and critical operations to compensate for information lost during compression. Experiments show that Memory consistently improves prompt-based Chain-of-Draft (CoD) compression across mathematical reasoning, complex reasoning, and science question answering tasks, yielding accuracy gains of 21.4, 28.0, 29.5, and 6.61 points over CoD on GSM8K, MATH, BBH, and MMLU-Sci, while achieving a 1.14--1.49$\times$ latency speedup over standard CoT. Memory is also compatible with token-level, reasoning-trace-level, and inference-state compression mechanisms. Further analyzes show that the gains come from relevant reasoning memories rather than simply increasing context length.

</details>


### [151] [Prompt-Model Interaction Reaches the Fixed Points: A deterministic, task-free structural readout -- and the factorizations of it that failed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21315)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过无任务读头（固定点结构）证明了提示词效应既非纯模型性质也非纯提示词性质，而是提示词-模型对（prompt-model pair）的交互属性，且该交互在短窗口下全幅出现，但无法由前缀长度、四类现象学因素或注意力汇聚机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证据显示提示词效果会随模型和格式变化，但这仅限于任务准确率读头，无法区分交互发生在任务机制层面还是条件分布本身层面。作者希望在一个无任务、确定性、只反映模型如何阅读片段的读头上检验这种交互是否存在及其强度。

Method: 使用短窗口argmax映射x_{t+1}=argmax_x p(x|x_{t-1},x_t)的固定点结构作为无任务读头，从96个起点普查（censused）固定点比例及固定点结构类。系统地检验了前缀长度单调性、四类现象学因素（散文vs标记、普适方向、双向性、指令抵抗）以及注意力汇聚机制。

Result: (1) 交互以全幅度到达该读头：9个token的条件化可使固定点比例跨越大部分取值范围、改变四类结构类并重排模型顺序；而相当于60.5 IFEval点的指令微调使结构类零移动。(2) 所提出的所有解释均失败：前缀长度效应非单调；四个现象学因素各自在提出后一次运行即被扩大样本推翻；注意力汇聚机制仅在5个模型中2个预测正确（等于随机水平），且在真实文本上成立、在均匀随机探测输入上失效——属于范式外而非范式内被证伪。一个固定9-token前缀将4个模型推向0、2个推向1；双向性在分布内起点上存续。

Conclusion: 在此读头上，解释的最小单位是提示词-模型对，而非提示词本身或模型本身。论文反复犯且被该读头捕获的错误是有名称的：把具有某种形状的标准（criterion with a shape）应用于没有变化余地的量（a quantity with no room to vary）。

Abstract: That a prompt's effect is not a property of the prompt is established: prompts optimised for one model degrade on another, and rankings reorder under neutral reformatting. That evidence is about task accuracy, which cannot say whether the interaction is a fact about task machinery or about the conditional distribution itself. We ask on a readout with no task in it: the fixed-point structure of the short-window argmax map x_{t+1} = argmax_x p(x | x_{t-1}, x_t), censused from 96 starts. It is deterministic, so nothing can be helped or hurt, and it exists only at short windows -- four of six models lose it entirely by window 16 -- so everything here concerns how a model reads a fragment. Two results. First, the interaction reaches this readout at full magnitude: nine tokens of conditioning move the fixed-point fraction across most of its range, change a four-way structural class, and reorder models, while instruction tuning worth 60.5 IFEval points moves the class by zero. Second, nothing we proposed carries it. Prefix length fails: the effect is not monotone. Four phenomenological factors -- prose-versus-markup, a universal direction, bidirectionality, instruct-resistance -- were each withdrawn within one run of being proposed, dissolved by widening the sample. And the nearest mechanistic account, attention-sink dominance of early tokens, predicts the sign of the shift on 2 of 5 models -- chance -- while a length-by-content cross shows it holds on real text and fails on our probe's uniformly random input, so we are outside its regime, not against it. One fixed nine-token prefix drives four models toward 0 and two toward 1; the bidirectionality survives in-distribution starts. On this readout the unit of explanation is the prompt-model pair. The recurring error it caught in us has a name: a criterion with a shape applied to a quantity with no room to vary.

</details>


### [152] [Move by Move: Measuring and Steering How LLMs Conduct Psychotherapy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21325)
*Afonso Baldo,Hugo Pitorro,Areti Vassilopoulos,Anabela C. Areias,Maya D'Eon,Fabíola Costa,Ricardo Rei,Nuno M. Guerreiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入了由十种治疗动作（therapeutic moves）组成的本体，基于MULTI-60清单构建，经五名持证心理学家注释验证，并采用与专家一致性相当的judge-based方法进行规模化标注。应用该本体比较真人临床医生与前沿模型在咨询转录和模型主导会话中的动作分布，发现模型过度使用探究（高达人类三倍），忽视心理教育，且表现出强烈的上下文锚定效应（承继而非主动发起策略）。将该本体作为工具集暴露给模型后，无需微调即可将动作分布平均偏差减半，回合级与人类治疗师的对齐度提升7-9个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户越来越依赖大语言模型获取情感支持，但业界对模型如何实际进行心理治疗互动知之甚少。缺乏系统性的分析框架来评估模型的心理治疗行为，因此需要建立一个可验证、可扩展的编码体系来刻画治疗动作。

Method: 1) 构建包含十种治疗动作的本体，基于MULTI-60清单提炼为紧凑的功能化类别；2) 通过五名持证心理学家的注释活动进行验证；3) 采用judge-based方法实现规模化标注，其一致性达到专家水平；4) 将本体应用于真实咨询转录和模型主导会话，比较人类临床医生与前沿模型的动作分布差异；5) 将本体作为工具集暴露给模型，观察其对动作分布和对齐度的影响。

Result: 模型在探究（inquiry）动作上的使用频率高达人类的三倍，忽视心理教育（psychoeducation）类动作，且表现出强烈的上下文锚定（context-anchored）特性——承继人类治疗师发起的策略但很少主动发起。将本体作为工具暴露给模型后，动作分布的平均偏差减半（与人类分布的距离大幅缩短），回合级与人类治疗师的对齐度提升7-9个百分点，且无需任何微调。

Conclusion: 基于本体驱动的工具暴露是一种有效且低成本的干预手段，能够显著改善模型在心理治疗场景中的行为对齐度。这提示模型的心理治疗能力短板（如过度探究、忽视心理教育）可以通过结构化知识注入而非参数更新来缓解，为提升LLM情感支持质量提供了新方向。

Abstract: Users increasingly turn to large language models for emotional support, yet little is known about how these models actually conduct a psychotherapy interaction. We introduce an ontology of ten therapeutic moves: compact, function-based categories grounded in the MULTI-60 inventory, validated through an annotation campaign with five licensed psychologists, and scaled with a judge-based approach that matches expert agreement. Applying it to real counseling transcripts and model-led sessions, we compare the move distributions between human clinicians and a panel of frontier models. Models over-use inquiry at up to three times the human rate, neglect psychoeducation, and are strongly context-anchored: they carry forward strategies initiated by a human clinician but rarely initiate them themselves. Exposing the ontology as a set of tools roughly halves the mean deviation from the human move distribution and improves turn-level alignment with human therapist by 7-9 percentage points, without any fine-tuning.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD)"范式，系统化地阐述了大语言模型驱动的智能体如何在规范驱动下重构软件开发生命周期（SDLC）。论文将SDAD定义为"上游严谨形式化+下游高速实现"的综合体，涵盖意图捕获、机器可读规范、智能体合成以及多智能体验证四阶段，并对比了Human-Agile（2020）与Agentic-SDAD（2026）两种范式在产物、节奏、问责机制和安全态势上的差异。文章进一步扩展到团队角色转型、量化治理指标（如Ambiguity Tax、Spec Fidelity、SER、TCI_agentic）及混合估算和分阶段迁移的实施路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码智能体已具备数十万至百万token的上下文窗口，能够一次性处理大量功能需求文档（FRD）和仓库上下文。这从根本上改变了SDLC的约束条件——规范质量成为自主交付的执行燃料。然而，现有软件开发方法论（如Waterfall和Agile）并未为这种agentic速度下的工程纪律提供理论框架，需要一种新的范式来协调规范精度与高速迭代之间的关系。

Method: 论文通过三个层面展开：(1) 范式形式化——将SDAD定义为四阶段流程（意图捕获、机器可读规范、智能体合成、独立多智能体验证+人工签核）；(2) 历史定位——重访Waterfall与Agile之间的钟摆运动，将AI编码确立为第四种生产范式；(3) 扩展模型——覆盖团队角色蜕变（工程师、QA、平台、产品）、量化治理指标体系（Ambiguity Tax衡量规范歧义、Spec Fidelity衡量规范保真度、SER衡量合成错误率、TCI_agentic含修复乘子phi衡量总变更成本），以及通过混合估算和分阶段迁移蓝图的落地策略。

Result: 论文综合了工业和学术界关于AI增强测试与验证的证据，论证了合成与发布授权相分离的必要性。核心结论是：智能体的速度并不会消除工程纪律，而是将纪律上游化——转移到规范精度、显式门禁和可审计溯源中。通过引入量化指标和迁移蓝图，论文为Agile到Agentic-SDAD的过渡提供了可操作的治理框架。

Conclusion: Agile的"响应变化"精神在Agentic时代不应被抛弃，但其执行方式需要被重新校准：变化应对的成本应在上游通过更高的规范精度来摊销。SDAD的核心主张是，agentic开发的高速度并非取代严谨性，而是将严谨性的位置从实现阶段前移至规范阶段，并通过显式的质量门禁和可审计溯源来保障交付可信度。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [154] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM编码代理每次会话从空上下文开始、丢弃先前积累知识的问题，提出了PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO)系统。该系统在启动时并行查询两个独立的内存后端（PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引），融合结果后通过文件系统注入将简报传递给新启动的Claude Code实例，并管理完整的代理生命周期。报告涵盖2025年12月至2026年3月四个月的部署经验，记录了三代上下文传递机制及其失败模式和工程权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理每次会话从空的上下文窗口开始，无法利用此前工作中积累的知识，导致效率低下和重复劳动。作者希望弥合这一"会话间记忆鸿沟"，让代理在每次启动时都能继承相关的历史知识。

Method: PAO在代理启动时并行查询两个独立内存后端：PostgreSQL实体-观测数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引，采用后端特定的检索策略融合结果。生成的简报通过文件系统注入利用宿主代理的配置自动读取行为传递给Claude Code。系统管理完整生命周期，包括信任预置（trust pre-seeding）、就绪轮询与错误检测、以及适应性终端文本注入。基于四个月部署经验迭代了三代上下文传递机制。

Result: 经过四个月（2025年12月至2026年3月）的常规部署，系统经历了三代上下文传递机制的设计迭代。每个机制均基于前一机制暴露的失败模式进行重构。报告还揭示了桥接异构内存系统而非构建统一系统所带来的工程权衡。

Conclusion: PAO验证了通过并行异构内存检索和文件系统注入为LLM编码代理提供会话间记忆的可行性。经验报告表明，使用现有异构存储系统而非构建统一内存系统是一种务实且可持续的工程路径，但其代价是需要处理多代机制演变中的兼容性和可靠性挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [155] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了LLM安全对齐的深层缺陷：安全机制仅在生成末期触发，而非真正消除预训练中习得的有害概念知识。研究发现存在一个"意图地平线"（约15-20%层深），在此处模型对有害意图的独特表征会因上下文适配而坍缩，使晚期层的对抗样本与安全查询在数学上不可区分。为此提出Latent Intent Verification (LIV)轻量探针防御方法，在不重训练模型的情况下，基于早期层保留的"有害签名"检测伪装攻击，在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上超越标准护栏20-50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的安全对齐机制存在根本性缺陷：安全机制仅在生成末尾触发拒绝，并未真正从模型中抹除有害概念知识。这种"架构断裂"使模型容易受到语义伪装（Semantic Camouflage）攻击——将有害意图包裹在良性叙述语境（如创意写作）中，从而绕过输入和输出的安全防护。

Method: 首先对三种SLM家族（Phi-3, Qwen2.5, Gemma-2b）在对抗压力下进行潜层激活轨迹分析，识别出通用的"Intent Horizon"——一个关键深度（通常为总层数的15-20%），在此处模型预训练的独特有害意图表征因将查询语境化为"安全"叙述而坍缩。基于此洞察，提出Latent Intent Verification (LIV)——一种轻量级探针防御方法，利用早期层中保留的独特可检测"有害签名"来识别伪装攻击。

Result: 实验结果显示：晚期层的伪装攻击表征与安全查询在数学上不可区分（检测率<20%），而早期层表征保留了可检测的"有害签名"。LIV在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上所有测试架构中比标准护栏表现优异20-50%，能有效中和零日语义攻击，且无需模型重训练。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了LLM安全对齐的"深度依赖"特性：安全对齐主要作用在模型后期层而早期层仍保留有害知识。通过利用早期层的信息，可以在不进行昂贵重训练的情况下构建高效、轻量的防御机制，为LLM安全领域提供了一种新的防御范式和理论框架。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [156] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了多模态在智能体框架中的影响，从感知、推理、规划、记忆和行动五个核心功能模块出发，分析了从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演变，并提出了以模态为中心的、将架构设计选择与智能体能力关联的分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型（LLM）智能体的综述已大量存在，但近年来多模态性如何塑造智能体（agency）的能力尚未被系统性地研究。随着大型多模态模型（LMMs）的兴起，智能体系统可以处理和整合图像、音频、视频等多种模态，提升其现实世界适用性，因此亟需填补这一研究空白。

Method: 本文采用以模态为中心的视角，从智能体框架的五个核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）系统地分析多模态的影响；通过委托（delegated）、晚期融合（late-fusion）和早期融合（early-fusion）三种架构范式考察模态集成方式；并提出了一个模态中心的分类法，将架构设计选择与智能体能力相关联，同时跨多个应用领域（机器人、GUI与网页导航、多媒体内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索）对现有工作进行组织与审查。

Result: 该综述追踪了从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演变，评估了由基础感知和多模态推理催生的智能体行为，并在多个应用领域分析了多模态智能体系统的性能表现，同时讨论了效率-可扩展性权衡（包括训练与推理成本、延迟和部署约束）。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦多模态性在智能体设计中的影响，本文旨在识别关键研究空白，并为构建健壮、通用的智能系统绘制路线图。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [157] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于树集成（Tree-based Ensembles）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，在保持高精度的同时提供人类可理解的解释。框架包括模态编码、概念聚类降维、树集成分类器（LDT、LDF、LDAB）以及改进的特征重要性度量。相比多模态Transformer，该方法在F1-mod上提升4.3%；相比可解释基线IMR，准确率提升3.0%；提出的特征重要性度量在人类标注一致性上显著优于默认重要性（IEMOCAP上62.2% vs 43.2%，CMU-MOSI上46.7% vs 32.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感与行为分类器需要同时兼顾竞争性精度与人类可理解的决策解释。现有高容量模型（尤其是Transformer）虽预测性能强，但其分布式表示和深度非线性使得难以对单个多模态特征赋予有意义的权重，限制了在临床情感监测、教育评估等信任敏感场景中的应用。

Method: 提出基于树集成的多模态分类框架：(1) 将每个模态编码为tokens；(2) 提取并聚类概念以降低维度；(3) 通过树集成分类器对融合模态进行路由分类；(4) 利用改进的特征重要性度量解释趋势。该度量在二分类任务中降低负类的权重影响，从而改善指示器/标志物检测。具体提出了三种树集成模型：LDT、LDF和LDAB。

Result: 所提出的树集成模型（LDT、LDF、LDAB）相比多模态Transformer在F1-mod上取得4.3%的提升，相比主要可解释多模态基线IMR在准确率上取得3.0%的提升。提出的多模态特征重要性在提取显著模态间概念时，相比默认特征重要性获得更高的人类标注一致性（IEMOCAP：62.2% vs 43.2%；CMU-MOSI：46.7% vs 32.1%）。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了树集成方法能够在多模态情感与行为分类中同时实现高精度和高可解释性，克服了Transformer在解释性上的局限，并为信任敏感应用（如临床情感监测和教学评估）提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal affect and behaviour classifiers that fuse heterogeneous text, audio, and visual streams must simultaneously achieve competitive accuracy and produce human-understandable explanations of the cues driving their decisions -- a dual objective that current high-capacity models, notably Transformers, only partially address. While Transformers attain strong predictive performance, their distributed representations and deep nonlinearity make it difficult to assign meaningful importance weights to individual multimodal features, limiting their use in trust-sensitive applications such as clinical affect monitoring and educational assessment. We address this gap by developing a framework based on tree-based ensembles that balances accuracy and interpretability. The framework encodes each modality into tokens, extracts and clusters concepts to reduce dimensionality, routes the fused modalities through tree-based ensemble classifiers, and interprets trends using a novel modified feature importance metric. The modified importance reduces the influence of the negative class in binary classification tasks, thereby improving indicator or marker detection. The proposed tree-based ensembles -- Linear Discriminant Tree (LDT), Linear Discriminant Forest (LDF), and Linear Discriminant AdaBoost (LDAB) -- achieve F1-mod gains of 4.3\% over the Multimodal Transformer and accuracy gains of 3.0\% over the primary interpretable multimodal baseline, Interpretable Multimodal Routing (IMR). The proposed multimodal feature importance extracts salient inter-modal concepts with substantially higher human-annotator agreement scores than default feature importance (62.2\% vs.\ 43.2\% on IEMOCAP; 46.7\% vs.\ 32.1\% on CMU-MOSI).

</details>


### [158] [Representation Affects Retrieval: A Case Study of Skill Discovery and Routing in a Multimodal Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20389)
*Kevin Dela Rosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过对生产级多模态视频代理系统Tinycloud的实例研究，探讨了LLM规划器在系统提示中如何表示和选择技能（工具技能与工作流技能）。在六项任务的选择消融实验中，发现完整自动加载在所有任务上都选对了目标技能，而关闭自动加载会导致发现失败。核心发现是：提示内技能暴露并非单调有益——部分暴露可能产生词法竞争，抑制正确选择，这与大规模场景中基于嵌入检索的技能路由形成对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产代理系统需要从不断增长的技能库中发现并排序出最适合用户任务的技能。小规模场景下，这个选择发生在上下文中（LLM规划器从系统提示中暴露的技能表示中直接选择），而无需显式的嵌入检索步骤。作者希望研究这种上下文内选择如何作为大规模嵌入检索的小N对应物，并通过生产系统案例理解技能表示的机制与局限。

Method: 以Tinycloud（生产多模态视频代理系统）为案例，将其技能划分为两类表示：工具技能（封装单一外部API或系统工具，作为原始词汇）和工作流技能（编排工具技能调用并渲染模板生成具名交付物）。通过两种暴露面呈现给规划器：内联体表面（完整指令、脚本、模板，用于自动加载技能）和单行列表（用于按需技能）。设计六项任务的选择消融实验，跨越三种暴露制度（全开、默认、全关）进行评估。

Result: 全自动加载（all-on）在每项任务上都选出了目标技能；全关（all-off）减慢了执行速度并产生难以发现的失败；生产默认设置（default）会因词法信号与自动加载的工具技能冲突而错误路由一项任务，将规划器注意力从已列出的工作流技能上转移。核心结果是提示内暴露并非单调有益，部分暴露可能产生词法竞争抑制正确选择。

Conclusion: 本文将小N（上下文内技能选择）的观察与大规模基于检索的技能路由工作联系起来，指出两者之间可能存在原理上的共通性。该贡献定位为案例研究而非基准测试，为理解生产级代理系统中技能表示与暴露策略的设计权衡提供实证参考。

Abstract: A production agent harness must discover and rank, from a growing library of skills, the one most appropriate for a user's task. At small scale this selection happens in context: the LLM planner chooses among skill representations exposed in its system prompt, without an explicit embedding-based retrieval step. We treat this in-context selection as the small-N counterpart to embedding-based skill retrieval at scale, and present a case study of how Tinycloud, a production multimodal video agent harness, represents its skills for the planner. The harness ships skills under two recurring representations: tool-skills that wrap a single external API or system tool and serve as primitive vocabulary, and workflow-skills that orchestrate tool-skill calls plus a template render to produce one named deliverable. The harness exposes them via two surfaces in the system prompt: an inlined-body surface (full instructions, scripts, templates) for autoloaded skills, and a one-line listing for on-demand skills. A six-task selection ablation across three exposure regimes (all-on, default, all-off) shows that full autoload selects the gold skill on every task; all-off slows execution and produces hard discovery failures; and the production default misroutes one task because its lexical signal collides with an autoloaded tool-skill that pulls planner attention away from a listed workflow-skill. The headline finding is that in-prompt exposure of skills is not monotonically helpful: partial exposure can create lexical competition that suppresses correct selection. We connect this small-N observation to recent retrieval-based skill-routing work at large scale, and frame this contribution as a case study rather than a benchmark.

</details>


### [159] [Nexus: Depth-Adaptive KV-Cache Splicing and Retrieval-Decoupled Tool Routing for Agentic LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20397)
*Mustafa Arslan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nexus针对MCP协议下Agentic LLM因工具schema过长导致的prefill延迟问题，提出双杠杆方案：一是通过INT8语义查找缓冲区(SLB)配合校准的交叉编码器边际门控，将工具路由与schema预填充成本解耦，用压缩文本签名(中位19 token)生成参数；二是将编译好的schema KV块移植到实时上下文中，并采用深度自适应后缀重解码修复RoPE相位漂移。实验表明Nexus在250个工具规模下路由准确率保持89%，首个参数token早1.66x到达，节省约80%主上下文token，同时提供1.1-1.7x的TTFT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在MCP协议下，Agentic LLM每个回合都要重新编码冗长的工具schema，而prefill的复杂度随序列长度呈二次方增长，导致当工具注册表变大时，TTFT(首个token时间)被prefill主导。这种计算瓶颈严重制约了工具型LLM代理的扩展性和响应速度，亟需解耦路由与schema编码成本的方案。

Method: Nexus采用双杠杆设计：(1)主要杠杆——INT8语义查找缓冲区(SLB)配合校准的交叉编码器边际门控从检索角度选择工具，参数生成基于压缩文本签名(中位19 token)而非拼接KV缓存，该路径与深度无关；(2)次要杠杆——将编译的schema KV块直接移植到实时上下文，但受RoPE相位漂移限制，超过阈值P=256需通过深度自适应后缀重解码修复接缝，必要时升级为完整的重新预填充。

Result: 在250个工具规模下路由准确率保持约89%，此时全schema拼接基线已溢出上下文窗口；首个参数token到达时间比全schema重新预填充快1.66x，节省约80%主上下文token；TTFT在中等深度下获得1.1-1.7x加速，在深上下文收敛至持平；never-regress属性保证输出保真度(顶层一致，D_KL≈0)，但延迟可能降至0.98x。

Conclusion: Nexus验证了通过语义检索实现工具路由与schema编码成本解耦的有效性，确立了"锚外RoPE保真度边界"和"参考自由漂移门预测失效"(Spearman rho=0.193)两个负面结果。所有定量指标来自单一模型配置(Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M)，定性边界可泛化，定量包络则依赖特定模型。

Abstract: Agentic large language models (LLMs) on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) re-encode verbose tool schemas every turn, so prefill - quadratic in sequence length - dominates time-to-first-token (TTFT) as the tool registry grows. Nexus's primary lever is to decouple routing from the schema-prefill cost: an INT8 semantic lookaside buffer (SLB) with a calibrated cross-encoder margin gate selects tools by retrieval, and arguments are generated over a compressed textual signature (median 19 tokens) rather than over spliced key/value (KV) cache. This path is depth-independent: routing accuracy stays near 89% as the registry scales to 250 tools - where a concatenate-all-schemas baseline overflows the context window entirely - and it reaches a first-argument token 1.66x sooner than a full-schema re-prefill at a ~80% main-context token saving. As a secondary, bounded lever we transplant a compiled schema KV block directly into the live context. This is fundamentally limited by rotary position embedding (RoPE) phase drift: an anchored splice is output-exact, but off-anchor placement corrupts attention, so beyond a threshold P=256 Nexus repairs the seam with a depth-adaptive suffix redecode that escalates to a full re-prefill. The resulting never-regress property is a guarantee on output fidelity (top-1 agreement, D_KL approx. 0) - not on latency, which can dip to 0.98x before converging to parity - alongside a 1.1-1.7x TTFT speedup at moderate depth that narrows to parity at deep context. Two negative results bound the design: the off-anchor RoPE fidelity boundary, and the failure of a reference-free drift gate to predict drift (Spearman rho = 0.193). All measurements are from one model tuple (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M) on Apple-silicon unified memory; the qualitative boundaries generalize, while the quantitative envelope is tuple-specific.

</details>


### [160] [Environmental Slow AI: Design Principles for Generative Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20398)
*Vanessa Utz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，从环境人文学传统审视当前生成式AI中的唯效率/最大化价值观，提出以环境可持续性为核心价值的"Slow AI"设计原则。提出五项设计原则（克制、充足性、选择性而非保留、物质可见性、摩擦即可供性），每项原则同时作用于设计实现层和阐释反思层，通过将反思性阐释内嵌于设计，恢复被无摩擦默认设置悄然剥夺的人类决策权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统在规模化生产文化制品的同时，其设计也反映了嵌入的文化价值观。一旦这些价值观被识别出来，就面临被有意重塑的可能性。作者认为当前生成式AI承载的是最大化价值观，而这一取向与生态可持续性相悖，因此需要从价值观层面系统性地提出替代性设计原则。

Method: 该文为征候性/立场性论证方法。以环境人文学传统为分析框架，批判当前生成式AI的最大化价值观；以Slow AI为统摄性术语（该术语已在多个研究和实践项目中流通），提出五项设计原则：克制、充足性、选择性优先于保留、物质可见性、摩擦作为可供性。每项原则在两层运作：设计实现层与阐释层（促使用户和开发者对系统进行反思性参与）。

Result: 形成了一套以环境可持续性为核心的五项设计原则框架。这些原则并非单向地限制系统，而是通过双重结构（设计实现+阐释反思）实现双重目标：既改变系统行为，又改变使用者与系统的关系。其效果是扩展人类能动性——恢复被无摩擦默认设置静默移除的决策机会，并将阐释性反思内建于设计之中。

Conclusion: 作者主张生成式AI的价值观并非不可改变，通过有意识的设计原则可以将可持续性确立为核心价值。Slow AI框架下的五项原则在技术和解释两个层面共同作用，不仅能使AI系统更具生态意识，还能通过设计内建的反思性来恢复和拓展人类决策空间，从而对抗无摩擦技术中隐含的价值侵蚀。

Abstract: Generative AI (genAI) systems produce cultural artefacts at scale, but they also reflect embedded cultural values through their design. Once identified, these values become open to deliberate reshaping. This position paper examines the maximalist values of current generative AI through an environmental humanities tradition and proposes design principles in which environmental sustainability serves as the core value instead. The principles are developed under the umbrella of Slow AI, a term that already circulates across several distinct research and practice programs. Five design principles are articulated (restraint, sufficiency, selectivity over retention, material visibility, and friction as affordance), each of them illustrated against the current design of widely deployed systems. Each principle operates at two levels: a design implementation, and an interpretive layer at which users and developers are prompted toward reflective engagement with the system. Together these principles extend human agency by restoring decisions that frictionless defaults have silently removed and do so by building interpretive reflection into design.

</details>


### [161] [When Retrieval Fails Before It Begins: Structurally Indirect Prerequisite Eviction as a Retention Failure in Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20400)
*Minkyu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了检索中心范式下"关键证据必然幸免于驱逐"的隐含假设，提出了一种检索前失败模式——"结构性间接先决条件驱逐"，并提供了其操作定义、可复现确定性基准和逐种子追踪诊断。作者提出依赖感知语义垃圾回收（DSGC）这一单跳图感知规则，在词级编码器下将全链保留率从0.03提升至0.90，在句级编码器下从0.23提升至1.00。鲁棒性检验进一步确定了单跳规则有效的预算与规模区间，为"先保留后检索"作为独立失败边界提供了机理分析支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体记忆研究集中于检索阶段，隐含假设被驱逐的证据在需要时必然仍存在。然而在固定预算（内存/上下文限制）下，驱逐策略可能导致与查询弱对齐但结构上必要的上游块被移除，从而在检索发生前就导致任务失败。作者旨在暴露并定义这一被忽视的检索前失败模式，并探索补救策略。

Method: 1) 形式化定义"结构性间接先决条件驱逐"失败模式，并构建可复现的确定性基准；2) 提供逐种子追踪诊断工具以定位失败位置；3) 提出依赖感知语义垃圾回收（DSGC），一种基于单跳图感知规则的保留策略，利用块间依赖关系决定驱逐优先级。

Result: 在主要测试套件中，DSGC将全链保留率（full-chain retention）从0.03提升至0.90（词级编码器）以及从0.23提升至1.00（句级编码器）。鲁棒性检验进一步揭示了单跳规则在何种预算和规模区间下保持有效或发生性能退化。

Conclusion: 保留阶段的驱逐策略是记忆系统中独立于检索的重要失败边界。依赖感知驱逐规则（如单跳图规则）在链式推理场景中可大幅改善保留率，但其有效性受预算和规模约束；发布流水线与失败事后分析（postmortem）为该机制的进一步机理研究提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Agentic memory under a fixed budget involves two stages: retention and retrieval. Existing retrieval-centered paradigms implicitly assume necessary evidence survives eviction, but we challenge this by isolating a pre-retrieval failure mode: structurally indirect prerequisite eviction, in which upstream blocks weakly aligned with the query are discarded under budget pressure. We provide an operational definition of this failure, a reproducible deterministic benchmark, and per-seed trace diagnostics. Finally, we evaluate Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection (DSGC), a one-hop graph-aware rule. In our main suite, DSGC improves full-chain retention from 0.03 to 0.90 under a lexical encoder and from 0.23 to 1.00 under a sentence encoder. Robustness checks then identify the budget and scaling regimes where the one-hop rule holds or degrades. Our released pipeline and failure postmortem support mechanistic analysis of retention before retrieval as a distinct failure boundary.

</details>


### [162] [World models of environment, agent and joint agent-environment systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20401)
*Manuel Baltieri,Filippo Torresan,Yivan Zhang,Alexander Boyd,Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出应从"通道"(channel)而非"变量"来区分世界模型，将世界模型分为环境通道、智能体通道和联合过程三类，并利用计算力学分别定义其规范预测模型(ε-转换器或ε-机器)。进一步构建了由闭环耦合诱导的规范支持受限环境模型和智能体模型，核心结构结论是：规范支持受限环境状态通过规范联合因果状态进行分解，其转移结构直接由联合模型诱导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型是模型化强化学习的核心组件，但现有讨论通常只关注模型预测什么变量(观测、奖励、状态、潜变量或信息状态)。作者认为存在一个更根本的区分维度——模型建模的是哪个"通道"，这一视角被现有文献忽视。

Method: 使用计算力学(computational mechanics)形式化定义三类通道的规范预测模型：环境通道(O|A)对应ε-转换器，智能体通道(A|O)对应ε-转换器，联合过程(A,O)等价于无输入通道对应ε-机器。在此基础上，通过闭环耦合构造支持受限(支持于实际交互延续)的规范环境模型和智能体模型。关键结构证明：支持受限环境状态分解通过联合因果状态，其转移结构由联合模型直接诱导。

Result: (1) 规范环境模型恢复标准预测性状态表示；(2) 智能体与联合系统有类似的对偶规范模型；(3) 规范支持受限环境状态通过规范联合因果状态因子化，转移结构由联合模型诱导；(4) 给出一个POMDP/控制器示例，其中非受限环境模型有无穷多状态，而耦合诱导的规范支持受限模型是有限的。

Conclusion: 该框架澄清了不同世界模型究竟"模型了什么"，以及耦合与支持受限如何改变其规范预测结构和复杂度。这为模型化强化学习中世界模型的选择和设计提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: World models are a central component of model-based reinforcement learning. They are usually discussed in terms of what variables they predict, such as observations, rewards, states, latent or information states. We argue that there is a prior distinction: which channel they model. We consider three cases: the environment channel $O_{:} \mid A_{:}$, the agent channel $A_{:} \mid O_{:}$, and the realised joint process $(A, O)_{:}$, equivalently viewed as a channel with no inputs. Using computational mechanics, we define canonical predictive models for these three cases as $ε$-transducers or $ε$-machines. Canonical environment models recover standard predictive state representations, while the other two give analogous notions of canonical models for the agent and the joint system. We then build canonical support-restricted environment and agent models induced by closed-loop coupling, whose predictive equivalences range over continuations supported by the realised interaction. The key structural result is that canonical support-restricted environment states factor through the canonical joint causal states, and their transition structure is induced directly from the joint model; the agent-side construction is dual. Finally, we give a POMDP/controller example in which the unrestricted environment model has infinitely many states while the canonical support-restricted model induced by the coupling is finite. The framework clarifies what different world models are models of, and how coupling and support restriction can change their canonical predictive structure and complexity.

</details>


### [163] [StateSight: Benchmarking Latent Spatial-State Reconstruction in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20414)
*Michelle Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StateSight基准，用于隔离评估视觉语言模型从单张图像重建潜在空间结构的能力。该基准包含三类程序化生成任务：立方体网络对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数和4邻域连通分量计数，每类300个单图提示词，具有确定性标注。实验显示GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet 5在所有任务上均显著低于人类基线，并引入配套数据集StateSight-Steps用于逐步视觉状态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态问答基准往往混合了感知、OCR、领域知识、语言先验和推理等多种能力，难以单独隔离评估视觉语言模型从单张图像中重建潜在空间结构的能力。作者希望通过一个纯净的、程序化生成的基准来填补这一评估空白。

Method: 设计三个程序化生成的任务族（立方体网络对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、4邻域连通分量计数），每类300个单图像提示词，使用确定性oracle标签和精确匹配评分。另外构建StateSight-Steps配套数据集，包含900个交错图像-文本示例和3600个确定性中间视觉状态，用于可推导的逐步分析。

Result: GPT-5.5在三个任务上分别取得59.3%、33.3%和28.3%的准确率；Claude Sonnet 5分别取得53.3%、18.7%和7.3%。30名参与者的人类基线在60个样本上全部任务均优于模型，平均准确率为80.8%、68.8%和64.3%。所有直接运行均无格式错误。可见推导分析识别出图像状态重建和推理过程中的系统性问题。

Conclusion: 结果表明，即使模型输出格式有效，也可能掩盖其无法恢复可验证视觉推理所需空间结构的事实。格式正确性不能等同于空间理解能力，现有视觉语言模型在纯粹的空间结构重建任务上与人类存在显著差距。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for multimodal question answering, yet their ability to reconstruct latent spatial structure from a single image remains difficult to isolate. Broad benchmarks often combine perception, optical character recognition, domain knowledge, linguistic priors, and reasoning in the same evaluation. We introduce StateSight, a procedurally generated benchmark for cube-net opposite-face reasoning, occluded cube-tower counting, and 4-neighbor connected-component counting. Each task family contains 300 single-image prompts with deterministic oracle labels and exact-match scoring. OpenAI GPT-5.5, using the API model identifier gpt-5.5, achieved 59.3%, 33.3%, and 28.3% accuracy across the three tasks, while Claude Sonnet 5 achieved 53.3%, 18.7%, and 7.3%. All final direct runs had zero format errors. A 30-participant human baseline on 60 items exceeded both models on every task, with mean accuracies of 80.8%, 68.8%, and 64.3%. Visible-derivation analysis identified recurring errors in image-state reconstruction and reasoning procedure. We also introduce StateSight-Steps, a companion dataset of 900 interleaved image-text examples and 3,600 deterministic intermediate visual states.
